Press Release article 1 of 5, Source: West Corporation, 299 words

Oct 30, 2026: ExlService Holdings EXL named an OpenAI Select Partner

NEW YORK, Oct. 29, 2026 -- EXL [NASDAQ: EXLS], a global data and AI company, announced that it has been named an OpenAI Select Partner within the OpenAI Partner Network.

The OpenAI Partner Network is a global program that equips partners with the resources, enablement, and support to build, sell, and deliver AI solutions with OpenAI. It brings together partners with deep industry expertise, delivery capabilities, and customer relationships while equipping them with resources, enablement, and support to help enterprises adopt OpenAI frontier models and products and turn them into measurable impact.

As an OpenAI Select Partner, EXL will work with OpenAI to help organizations build, deploy, and scale AI responsibly. This work will help organizations put OpenAI frontier models to work across their business, using deeper reasoning and computer use to complete workflows and deliver finished work.

"Being named an OpenAI Select Partner reflects how we work with clients: combining frontier AI with deep understanding of their business to deliver outcomes," said Vishal Chhibbar, executive vice president and chief revenue officer, EXL. "Clients trust us to apply that capability where it matters most, across entire journeys and with accountability for the end result. That trust, built over years inside their operations is what turns AI ambition into results."

EXL brings together data, context and AI to help organizations in insurance, financial services, healthcare and life sciences, and other heavily regulated, operationally complex industries move AI from pilot to production in core enterprises. Its work includes helping insurers reimagine underwriting, claims management and administrative workflows; supporting health plans integration of AI payment integrity and patient engagement systems; and transforming risk and compliance, credit decisioning and customer engagement initiatives for financial service leaders.

Press Release article 3 of 5, Source: West Corporation, 305 words

Oct 27, 2026: ExlService Holdings EXL Reports 2026 Third Quarter Results

2026 Third Quarter Revenue of $634.0 Million, up 19.7% year-over-year

Q3 Diluted EPS (GAAP) of $0.45, up 25.3% from $0.36 in Q3 of 2025

Q3 Adjusted Diluted EPS (Non-GAAP) (1) of $0.62, up 28.5% from $0.48 in Q3 of 2025

NEW YORK, Oct. 27, 2026 -- ExlService Holdings, Inc. (NASDAQ: EXLS), a global data and AI company, today announced its financial results for the quarter ended September 30, 2026.

Chairman and Chief Executive Officer Rohit Kapoor said, "We entered 2026 with strong momentum that has accelerated through the year, delivering third quarter revenue growth of 20% year-on-year and adjusted diluted EPS growth of 29% year-on-year. Every segment of our business grew more than 15% organically. Data and AI-led revenue, almost two-thirds of our business, grew 36% on a reported basis and 31% organically. These results reflect the strength of our data and AI strategy and our differentiated market position: deep domain expertise combined with data and AI capabilities that help clients make AI work effectively across the enterprise. Our business model affords us very good visibility into the balance of the year, and we look forward to a solid finish to 2026."

Chief Financial Officer Maurizio Nicolelli said, "Our strong third quarter performance gives us the confidence to raise our full-year outlook once again. We now expect full-year 2026 total revenue to be in the range of $2.430 billion to $2.440 billion, up from our prior guidance of $2.390 billion to $2.415 billion, reflecting 16% to 17% year-over-year growth on a reported basis and 15% on an organic constant currency basis. We now expect adjusted diluted earnings per share of $2.34 to $2.36, a 20% to 21% increase over 2025, up from our prior guidance of $2.25 to $2.29."

Press Release article 4 of 5, Source: West Corporation, 350 words

Oct 26, 2026: ExlService Holdings iMerit, an EXL Company,launches iMerit Scholars platform as AI talent war shifts to domain expertise

New platform connects domain experts with mission-critical AI work as companies compete for the human expertise needed to move AI from innovation to execution

NEW YORK, Oct. 26, 2026 -- iMerit, an EXL company, today announced the launch of its iMerit Scholars platform, designed to seamlessly manage hundreds of thousands of global domain experts (Scholars) who work onfine-tuning, evaluation and validation of generative AI models. This represents the first major launch since EXL (Nasdaq: EXLS) acquired iMerit in July to accelerate the progression of frontier AI into regulated enterprises.

The launch comes as the AI talent war heats up across generative and physical AI. The iMerit Scholars program already brings together a curated global community of 30,000 domain experts and linguists to support advanced model training, evaluation, validation and safety across specialized fields. The new iMerit Scholars platform provides a unified system to recruit, onboard, curate, assign, manage, engage, pay, and retain these experts at scale. It is powered by iMerit's proprietary Ango Hub, which allows customers and experts to collaborate on complex multimodal data to generate highly curated and validated training artifacts for high-stakes models.

"iMerit has always differentiated itself by a curated, people-first approach. We're seeing that become even more important as we scale up to help the frontier labs," said Radha Basu, executive vice president, head of iMerit at EXL. "The next phase of the GenAI race is about creating a painless and supportive infrastructure so that these highly capable specialists can enjoy their work and give their best. Scaling high quality and high engagement is mainly a human capital opportunity, backed by enabling technology. The iMerit Scholars platform is designed to create a seamless experience for top-notch talent to continue building and improving the next generation of AI."

"Now, as a part of EXL, we are expanding from the creation of models in frontier labs to the implementation of model workflows in highly regulated enterprises in banking, healthcare and insurance.

Press Release article 5 of 5, Source: West Corporation, 298 words

Oct 09, 2026: ExlService Holdings EXL transforms client workflows with the integration of agentic AI into core insurance and healthcare solutions

New agentic AI capabilities compress life and annuities insurance product launch cycles, optimize in-force policy management, and move health plan payment integrity upstream

NEW YORK, Oct. 08, 2026 -- EXL [NASDAQ: EXLS], a global data and AI company, announced it has integrated agentic AI across core solutions for insurance and healthcare, combining workflow orchestration, enterprise data intelligence and AI-powered automation to modernize operations and deliver better experiences for its clients. As part of its portfolio of integrated AI-led solutions, EXL is embedding AI agents directly into the offerings where its clients already work. This includes reimagining two of EXL's industry-leading solutions, EXL LifePRO and EXL Payment Integrity, with AI-powered automation to help modernize operations and deliver better outcomes.

EXL LifePRO Agentic Suite addresses a central challenge confronting the life insurance industry: slow product launch cycles. Most life insurers still need eight to ten months to bring a new product to market, a constraint that limits how many products a carrier can launch each year and how quickly it can respond to regulatory or competitive changes. According to a 2025 LIMRA and Bain & Company study, approximately 100 million Americans say they need life insurance but haven't purchased it, yet just 9.4 million policies were sold in 2024. Carriers are largely competing for the same buyers, leaving a large gap in demand unaddressed.

By automating manual, repetitive workflows, EXL LifePRO Agentic Suite compresses the product launch cycle to as few as 16 weeks, leveraging AI agents to ingest product specifications, interpret rules and rates, and propose executable configurations so teams can focus on reviewing and approval rather than build from scratch.

Oct 30, 2026: ExlService Holdings EXL named an OpenAI Select Partner

NEW YORK, Oct. 29, 2026 -- EXL [NASDAQ: EXLS], a global data and AI company, announced that it has been named an OpenAI Select Partner within the OpenAI Partner Network.

The OpenAI Partner Network is a global program that equips partners with the resources, enablement, and support to build, sell, and deliver AI solutions with OpenAI. It brings together partners with deep industry expertise, delivery capabilities, and customer relationships while equipping them with resources, enablement, and support to help enterprises adopt OpenAI frontier models and products and turn them into measurable impact.

As an OpenAI Select Partner, EXL will work with OpenAI to help organizations build, deploy, and scale AI responsibly. This work will help organizations put OpenAI frontier models to work across their business, using deeper reasoning and computer use to complete workflows and deliver finished work.

"Being named an OpenAI Select Partner reflects how we work with clients: combining frontier AI with deep understanding of their business to deliver outcomes," said Vishal Chhibbar, executive vice president and chief revenue officer, EXL. "Clients trust us to apply that capability where it matters most, across entire journeys and with accountability for the end result. That trust, built over years inside their operations is what turns AI ambition into results."

EXL brings together data, context and AI to help organizations in insurance, financial services, healthcare and life sciences, and other heavily regulated, operationally complex industries move AI from pilot to production in core enterprises. Its work includes helping insurers reimagine underwriting, claims management and administrative workflows; supporting health plans integration of AI payment integrity and patient engagement systems; and transforming risk and compliance, credit decisioning and customer engagement initiatives for financial service leaders.

Oct 27, 2026: ExlService Holdings EXL Reports 2026 Third Quarter Results

2026 Third Quarter Revenue of $634.0 Million, up 19.7% year-over-year

Q3 Diluted EPS (GAAP) of $0.45, up 25.3% from $0.36 in Q3 of 2025

Q3 Adjusted Diluted EPS (Non-GAAP) (1) of $0.62, up 28.5% from $0.48 in Q3 of 2025

NEW YORK, Oct. 27, 2026 -- ExlService Holdings, Inc. (NASDAQ: EXLS), a global data and AI company, today announced its financial results for the quarter ended September 30, 2026.

Chairman and Chief Executive Officer Rohit Kapoor said, "We entered 2026 with strong momentum that has accelerated through the year, delivering third quarter revenue growth of 20% year-on-year and adjusted diluted EPS growth of 29% year-on-year. Every segment of our business grew more than 15% organically. Data and AI-led revenue, almost two-thirds of our business, grew 36% on a reported basis and 31% organically. These results reflect the strength of our data and AI strategy and our differentiated market position: deep domain expertise combined with data and AI capabilities that help clients make AI work effectively across the enterprise. Our business model affords us very good visibility into the balance of the year, and we look forward to a solid finish to 2026."

Chief Financial Officer Maurizio Nicolelli said, "Our strong third quarter performance gives us the confidence to raise our full-year outlook once again. We now expect full-year 2026 total revenue to be in the range of $2.430 billion to $2.440 billion, up from our prior guidance of $2.390 billion to $2.415 billion, reflecting 16% to 17% year-over-year growth on a reported basis and 15% on an organic constant currency basis. We now expect adjusted diluted earnings per share of $2.34 to $2.36, a 20% to 21% increase over 2025, up from our prior guidance of $2.25 to $2.29."

New platform connects domain experts with mission-critical AI work as companies compete for the human expertise needed to move AI from innovation to execution

NEW YORK, Oct. 26, 2026 -- iMerit, an EXL company, today announced the launch of its iMerit Scholars platform, designed to seamlessly manage hundreds of thousands of global domain experts (Scholars) who work onfine-tuning, evaluation and validation of generative AI models. This represents the first major launch since EXL (Nasdaq: EXLS) acquired iMerit in July to accelerate the progression of frontier AI into regulated enterprises.

The launch comes as the AI talent war heats up across generative and physical AI. The iMerit Scholars program already brings together a curated global community of 30,000 domain experts and linguists to support advanced model training, evaluation, validation and safety across specialized fields. The new iMerit Scholars platform provides a unified system to recruit, onboard, curate, assign, manage, engage, pay, and retain these experts at scale. It is powered by iMerit's proprietary Ango Hub, which allows customers and experts to collaborate on complex multimodal data to generate highly curated and validated training artifacts for high-stakes models.

"iMerit has always differentiated itself by a curated, people-first approach. We're seeing that become even more important as we scale up to help the frontier labs," said Radha Basu, executive vice president, head of iMerit at EXL. "The next phase of the GenAI race is about creating a painless and supportive infrastructure so that these highly capable specialists can enjoy their work and give their best. Scaling high quality and high engagement is mainly a human capital opportunity, backed by enabling technology. The iMerit Scholars platform is designed to create a seamless experience for top-notch talent to continue building and improving the next generation of AI."

"Now, as a part of EXL, we are expanding from the creation of models in frontier labs to the implementation of model workflows in highly regulated enterprises in banking, healthcare and insurance.

Aug 18, 2026: EXL closes new $1 billion senior secured credit facility

Exhibit 99.1

EXL closes new $1 billion senior secured credit facility

NEW YORK Aug. 18, 2026 EXL (NASDAQ: EXLS), a global data and AI company, announced the closing of a new credit facility with PNC Bank, N.A., as Administrative Agent, and a syndicate of lenders that allows for borrowings of up to $1 billion. Bank of America, N.A., JPMorgan Chase Bank, N.A., and TD Bank, N.A. acted as joint lead arrangers on this transaction.

The facility increases EXLs borrowing capacity from the previous limit of $600 million and provides greater covenant flexibility to support the companys business strategy. The five-year senior secured credit agreement includes a $400 million term loan, permits revolver borrowings of up to $600 million, and has an accordion feature that would allow the facility to expand equal to the greater of $470 million or 100% of EBITDA for the trailing four quarters. The term of the new agreement will expire on August 18, 2031.

This deal reflects the confidence our banking partners have in EXLs financial strength and the long-term trajectory of our business, said Maurizio Nicolelli, chief financial officer of EXL. We have consistently prioritized a strong balance sheet, and this expanded debt capacity gives us the flexibility to extend our competitive advantage through targeted mergers and acquisitions while continuing to return capital to our shareholders under our $500 million share repurchase authorization. The closing of this facility is a clear demonstration of the focused execution against our capital allocation strategy.

August 18 2026: ExlService Holdings Inc Secures $1 Billion Financing Package with PNC Bank

ExlService Holdings, Inc. has entered into a significant financing agreement with PNC Bank, National Association, that includes a $600 million revolving credit facility and a $400 million term loan facility. The agreement, finalized on 18th August 2026, positions ExlService to enhance its liquidity and financial flexibility for future initiatives.

Under the new Credit Agreement, which matures on 18th August 2031, ExlService Holdings will benefit from a comprehensive $600 million revolving credit facility, which includes a $50 million swingline loan subfacility and a $20 million letter of credit subfacility. The term loan facility will also provide ExlService with additional capital, allowing it to strategically invest in growth opportunities while maintaining a strong balance sheet.

The terms of the Credit Agreement allow ExlService Holdings to adjust borrowing rates based on its Total Net Leverage Ratio, with interest rates ranging from 1.00% to 1.75% per annum depending on the chosen benchmark. This flexibility is designed to align with the company’s financial performance, ensuring cost-effective access to capital as needed.

As part of this financial restructuring, ExlService Holdings has used proceeds from the new credit facilities to fully repay its previous obligations under an agreement with Citibank, which included a $500 million revolving credit facility and a $100 million term loan facility. This transition was executed without incurring early termination penalties, allowing ExlService to streamline its debt obligations.

The new financing arrangement reflects ExlService Holdings' commitment to maintaining strong financial health, supported by a robust interest coverage ratio requirement of at least 3.00 to 1.00. The company is also permitted to increase its borrowing capacity under certain conditions, which could further enhance its operational capabilities and strategic growth initiatives in the coming years.

Blackstone Launches Senior Notes Offering

New York, October 27, 2026Blackstone (NYSE: BX) today announced its intention to offer, subject to market and other conditions, senior notes of Blackstone Reg Finance Co. L.L.C., its indirect subsidiary. The notes are to be fully and unconditionally guaranteed by Blackstone Inc. and its indirect subsidiaries, Blackstone Holdings I L.P., Blackstone Holdings AI L.P., Blackstone Holdings II L.P., Blackstone Holdings III L.P. and Blackstone Holdings IV L.P. Press Release article 4 of 5, Source: Blackstone, 276 words

Oct 01, 2026: Blackstone Announces Significant Investment to Launch Falcata, Leader in Next-Generation Interceptor Technologies

Blackstone today announced a significant investment to launch Falcata, a defense technology company formed through the acquisition and combination of TSC and Applied Systems Engineering, Inc. (ASEI) under a unified platform. Falcata brings together complementary technologies across the interceptor guidance chain to strengthen Americas industrial capacity and help meet the growing demand for cost-effective national security missions.

The formation of Falcata comes as the United States and its allies confront a rapidly changing threat environment in which inexpensive drones, missiles and other weapons can be deployed at significant scale. Recent conflicts have demonstrated the need for defensive systems that can deliver both effectiveness and volume, increasing demand not only for more affordable interceptors, but also for the critical technologies required to produce them.

Falcata will build on the existing capabilities, customer relationships and programs of both companies while investing in additional technology, manufacturing capacity and talent. The combined company brings together expertise across advanced seekers and radar technologies; guidance electronics and control actuation systems; GPS-denied navigation; ISR; launchers; and related technologies supporting missile defense and other national security missions.

The United States needs a defense industrial base capable of combating the evolving threats facing our warfighters, said Jonathan Moneymaker, Chairman of Falcata. Recent conflicts have demonstrated that lower-cost interceptors are a cornerstone of our national security strategy. Producing more interceptors requires greater capacity across critical sensing, guidance, navigation and control technologies inside them. We are building Falcata to deliver the technology America and its allies need at the scale the mission demands.

3.2 PAST YEAR TOP 10 STORIES

Story 1 of 10, Source: HourlyBusinessNews, 73 words

Blackstone Reaches Record $1.26 Trillion in Assets Under Management Amid Strong Fundraising Momentum

Blackstone 's Q3 2025 earnings report revealed record assets under management of $1.26 trillion, a 12% year-over-year increase, driven by $54.2 billion in quarterly inflows. Distributable earnings reached $1.89 billion, significantly exceeding analyst expectations. The firm's growth was supported by its expanding perpetual capital base of $500 billion and strong performance in its Credit & Insurance and Private Equity segments. Strategic investments in AI infrastructure and energy transition were key drivers of fundraising success.

Story 2 of 10, Source: HourlyBusinessNews, 72 words

Blackstone Commits $25+ Billion to Transform Pennsylvania's Digital and Energy Infrastructure Ecosystem

Blackstone announced a landmark $25 billion investment in Pennsylvania's digital and energy infrastructure, a commitment expected to catalyze a total of $85 billion in capital. The initiative will focus on data center development, power generation, and grid modernization. This project addresses the rising demand from AI and electrification and aims to create an integrated ecosystem that supports both digital growth and energy stability within the PJM Interconnection, North America's largest electric grid.

Story 3 of 10, Source: HourlyBusinessNews, 67 words

Blackstone's $24 Billion AirTrunk Acquisition Positions Firm as World's Largest Data Center Operator

In December 2024, Blackstone completed its $24 billion acquisition of AirTrunk, making it the world's largest data center operator by capital deployment. The deal provides Blackstone with a significant footprint in the fast-growing Asia-Pacific market, with AirTrunk operating 800 megawatts of capacity and holding land for over 1 gigawatt of future development. This move positions Blackstone to meet the escalating demand for AI infrastructure in the region.

Story 4 of 10, Source: HourlyBusinessNews, 59 words

Blackstone Q3 2025 Earnings Significantly Exceed Consensus as Distributable Earnings Reach $1.52 Per Share

Blackstone reported Q3 2025 distributable earnings of $1.52 per share, a 48% year-over-year increase that far surpassed analyst consensus. Total distributable earnings hit $1.99 billion, fueled by strong performance across all segments, particularly Credit & Insurance. Fee-related earnings grew 9% to $1.48 billion, and significant contributions from realized performance revenues highlighted the success of the firm's portfolio monetization strategies.

Story 5 of 10, Source: HourlyBusinessNews, 58 words

Private Equity Realizations Exceed $20 Billion as Blackstone Signals "Deal Dam Breaking" in Exit Environment

Blackstone's private equity realizations surpassed $20 billion in 2025, indicating a significant improvement in the exit environment. Management noted a "deal dam breaking" as the firm executed $30.6 billion in realizations across all asset classes in Q3 alone. This acceleration, driven by favorable market conditions, has increased distributable capital for investors and provided fresh capital for new opportunities.

Story 6 of 10, Source: HourlyBusinessNews, 52 words

Blackstone Strategic Partners Closes Record $5.5 Billion Infrastructure Secondaries Fund

Blackstone announced the final closing of a record-setting $5.5 billion infrastructure secondaries fund, Strategic Partners Infrastructure IV. This successful fundraising reflects strong institutional investor demand for acquiring mature infrastructure assets through secondary markets. The fund will target opportunities in sectors like energy, digital infrastructure, and transportation, leveraging Blackstone's extensive platform and relationships.

Story 7 of 10, Source: HourlyBusinessNews, 60 words

Blackstone Energy Transition Partners IV Achieves $5.6 Billion Final Close at Hard Cap

Blackstone closed its fourth energy transition fund, BEP IV, at its $5.6 billion hard cap, underscoring strong investor conviction in the sector. The fund targets investments in electricity demand growth, grid modernization, and renewable energy infrastructure. Having already committed over $27.5 billion to energy transition investments, this fund solidifies the theme as a core part of Blackstone's private equity strategy.

Story 8 of 10, Source: HourlyBusinessNews, 64 words

Blackstone Acquires Shermco for $1.6 Billion, Bolstering Energy Transition Portfolio with Electrical Infrastructure Services

Blackstone acquired Shermco Industries, a leading North American provider of electrical equipment services, for approximately $1.6 billion. The acquisition, made through its energy transition fund, aligns with the firm's focus on electrification and grid reliability. Shermco provides essential maintenance and testing services to data centers and utilities, offering exposure to a sector with recurring revenue and strong growth prospects driven by rising electricity demand.

Story 9 of 10, Source: HourlyBusinessNews, 60 words

Blackstone Acquires Energy Analytics SaaS Platform Enverus for Estimated $6+ Billion

Blackstone agreed to acquire Enverus, a leading energy data analytics SaaS platform, in a deal valued at over $6 billion. Enverus provides critical data and market intelligence to thousands of customers across the energy sector. The acquisition supports Blackstone's investment thesis in the energy transition, as Enverus's platform is essential for navigating market complexities and making informed capital allocation decisions.

Story 10 of 10, Source: HourlyBusinessNews, 58 words

Blackstone Private Wealth Platform Accelerates to $290 Billion AUM, Tripling Growth Over Five Years

Blackstone's Private Wealth platform reached approximately $290 billion in assets under management, tripling its size in five years. The platform saw $11 billion in net inflows in Q3 2025, reflecting strong demand from high-net-worth individuals for access to alternative assets. The growth is part of Blackstone's strategy to democratize alternative investments through tailored products and expanded distribution channels.

SECTION 9 CORPORATE PROFILE

9.1 Activities

Blackstone Inc., a global leader in alternative asset management and listed on the New York Stock Exchange, specializes in private equity, real estate, credit, and hedge fund investments. Founded in 1985, the firm manages a diverse portfolio of assets for institutional and individual investors, focusing on sourcing, executing, and overseeing investments that deliver long-term value across sectors like infrastructure and growth equity. Its core activities include raising capital, making strategic investments, and providing advisory services to enhance portfolio performance. Among its fastest-growing segments are private credit and real estate, fueled by rising demand for alternative financing solutions and urbanization trends, respectively. This expansion has positioned Blackstone as a key player in global markets, with a strong track record of innovation and operational excellence. It is the 3rd largest Asset manager company by market capitalization among NYSE- and TXSE-listed stocks.

Advsr AI Spotlight

Highlighting notable AI moves at the intersection of strategic operating companies and emerging startups. Big ideas in the works.

Strategic Deployments of AI

ACCESS Newswire, a communications technology company, launched SPOT by ACCESS Newswire, a suite of seven AI agents that check a press release in the Optimize step of the ACCESS Newswire platform, after it is written and before it is submitted

The University of Missouri-St. Louis and Lindbergh Schools partnered with Scale AI, a business-led consortium that supports business building and accelerating the adoption of AI-powered technologies, to launch Fundamentals of AI for Educators, a free, 12-hour online AI literacy course designed for K-12 teachers nationwide

Clarity AI, a global technology platform for extra-financial intelligence, launched its Transition Credibility Assessment to help investors evaluate whether companies are making progress towards their climate goals

Meta launched Style Sketch, an AI fashion app that helps users put together outfit ideas using clothes they already own, connecting to Instagram to build a virtual closet from photos users have posted, or letting them add items manually by snapping a photo, recording a quick video, or uploading images from your camera roll

Anthropic launched new tools for Claude Enterprise: smart reports, which show what that work is, and where that work is happening; in-app surveys that capture their feedback about how Claude is enabling them to do new work; and pooled group budgets that let admins set one shared budget per team

Bahrain Mumtalakat Holding Company, the sovereign wealth fund of the Kingdom of Bahrain, and ARP Group, the holding company of ARP Global Capital and ARP Digital, partnered to establish an AI-native and blockchain enabled wholesale bank

ZKH, a maintenance, repair, and operations procurement service platform in China, announced that its AI subsidiary, LingLong Intelligence, partnered with Intel to launch LingLong Insight 1.0, a lightweight industrial multimodal AI model that brings image and text understanding as well as intelligent verification to industrial worksites

Cal State LA and OFFIS Institute for Information Technology, a research institute in Oldenburg, Germany, partnered to establish a new research and academic partnership focused on artificial intelligence, geospatial analysis, and the resilience of critical infrastructure

Este Lauder expanded its partnership with Microsoft to launch its Unified Data Platform, built on Microsoft technology, which connects 80 years of consumer insights across Este Lauder's brands, regions, channels and functions to accelerate product innovation and spur sharper data-driven insights

NVIDIA and Meta expanded their partnership on Muse and the Muse family of models to improve inference and training performance, reflecting popular demand for useful AI agents

Tencent Cloud, the cloud business of global technology company Tencent, partnered with OmniOps, Saudi Arabia's sovereign AI infrastructure technology company, to build a reliable and scalable foundation for enterprise AI development and deployment across the Kingdom

Cognizant and Travelport expanded their partnership to build intelligent travel retail for the AI era

CrowdStrike and CoreWeave expanded their partnership, with CoreWeave bringing CrowdStrike Falcon platform protection and 24/7 managed security expertise to customers running mission-critical AI workloads on its cloud

Lumen Technologies and Microsoft announced an expanded co-selling collaboration to help enterprises overcome a critical challenge in AI adoption: connecting the data, applications and infrastructure that power AI workloads

Google expanded its partnership with Singapore to deploy frontier AI across the nation's healthcare system, with goals of accelerating scientific discoveries, transforming clinical care delivery, and empowering Singaporeans with proactive health tools

Tencent and Visa expanded their partnership to explore how emerging technologies, including AI and agentic capabilities, can support more seamless and secure digital commerce experiences

Strategic and AI Startup Collaborations

AudienceProject, a provider of independent cross-media measurement, partnered with xpln.ai, an attention intelligence platform helping advertisers measure, understand, and optimise attention across media environments, to provide customers with insights into the attentive reach of their campaigns across channels, allowing advertisers and agencies to make more confident investment decisions

Ebix, which operates AI-driven, API-first digital marketplaces and enterprise platforms across insurance, FinTech and health industries, partnered with intentyx, a deep-tech cybersecurity and AI observability company, to bring AI agent security and governance to regulated industries

Webull, a digital investment platform built on global infrastructure and AI technologies, and Sentinel AI, a retail sentiment and narrative intelligence platform, partnered on a joint pilot program that gives a listed company a direct channel to retail investors through their page on Webullʼs Corporate Connect Service

Akuna Learning, part of The Akuna Group, a broadcasting company that provides marketing, promotion, and content creation services for media sectors, partnered with Google to open applications for a free AI filmmaking programme targeting aspiring and emerging creators in Ghana, Kenya, Nigeria, Sierra Leone and South Africa

Digital Tails Group, a provider of 3D, AI, and immersive XR solutions for product visualization, launched AI Media Generation in the 3D configurator it built for CARVIDO, a maker of custom built-in wardrobes, so customers who design a wardrobe online can now see their exact configuration in a high-fidelity interior visualization

FORVIA, a global automotive supplier, and Raiqon GmbH, a specialist in scalable AI solutions for regulated engineering environments, partnered to advance the use of artificial intelligence in automotive engineering

Go.AI, a provider of on-premises, examiner-ready AI infrastructure for regulated industries, partnered with the Loyola University Chicago Ramblers to be its first jersey patch sponsor

Emergys, a digital transformation services company, partnered with enterprise AI platform Glean to help enterprises move AI and agentic systems from pilot to production

AI Startup Acquisitions

Oddball, an AI-powered Federal digital services provider, acquired the Salesforce Technology and Services Business of Synaptic Era

Omen AI, a startup bringing continuous fluid testing directly into AI data center cooling systems, acquired Aquatic Labs, a developer of water monitoring sensor technology designed for environmental measurement and aquatic data collection

Marvell, a company that manufactures and offers integrated circuit solutions, acquired Lumentum, a provider of optical and photonic technologies that power the networks and infrastructure behind AI, cloud computing and next-generation communications

Deepgram, a voice AI infrastructure company built for the demands of real-world conversation, acquired Daily, creators of Pipecat, an open-source voice agent framework

RapidCanvas, a managed AI execution company, acquired Entytle, the company behind the Autonomous Aftermarket for B2B OEMs, whose AI agents find and capture untapped revenue across an OEM's installed base

RWS, a global AI solutions company, acquired Acogroup, the parent company of Acolad, a global provider of language and content services

Avocado Consulting, an end-to-end project delivery consultancy, acquired AI Consulting Group, a provider of data and AI consultancy services

Vaisala, a provider of measurement instruments and intelligence for climate action, acquired AI weather company Atmo

AI Outtakes

Dartmouth College Provost Santiago Schnell resigned after a faculty committee found he used artificial intelligence to shape his writing, including in a guest column in The Washington Post on AI cheating. Schnell said he used ChatGPT to 'refine a few arguments' but that the column was based on his 'own thinking,' though the college paper, The Dartmouth, also found high rates of AI use in his recent writing

Volatility & Risk

Earnings and Valuation

This table compares TaskUs and Robert Half"s top-line revenue, earnings per share (EPS) and valuation.

Profitability

This table compares TaskUs and Robert Half's net margins, return on equity and return on assets.

Institutional & Insider Ownership

About TaskUs

( Get Free Report)

TaskUs, Inc. provides digital outsourcing services for companies in Philippines, the United States, India, and internationally. It offers digital customer experience that consists of omni-channel customer care services primarily delivered through non-voice digital channels; and other solutions, including experience and customer care services for new product or market launches, and customer acquisition solutions. The company also provides trust and safety solutions, such as review and disposition of user and advertiser generated visual, text, and audio content, which include removal or labeling of policy violating, and offensive or misleading content, as well as risk management, compliance, identity management, and fraud services; and artificial intelligence (AI) solutions that consist of data labeling, annotation, context relevance, and transcription services for training and tuning machine learning algorithms that enables to develop AI systems. It serves clients in various industry segments comprising e-commerce, FinTech, food delivery and ride sharing, gaming, technology, HealthTech, social media, and streaming media. The company was formerly known as TU TopCo, Inc. and changed its name to TaskUs, Inc. in December 2020. TaskUs, Inc. was founded in 2008 and is headquartered in New Braunfels, Texas.

About Robert Half

( Get Free Report)

Robert Half Inc. provides talent solutions and business consulting services in North America, South America, Europe, Asia, and Australia. The company operates through Contract Talent Solutions, Permanent Placement Talent Solutions, and Protiviti segments. The Contract Talent Solutions segment provides contract engagement professionals in the fields of finance and accounting, technology, marketing and creative, legal and administrative, and customer support. This segment markets its services to clients and employment candidates through both national and local advertising activities, including radio, digital advertising, job boards, alliance partners, and events. The Permanent Placement Talent Solutions segment engages in the placement of full-time accounting, finance, and tax and accounting operations personnel. The Protiviti segment offers consulting services in the areas of internal audit, technology consulting, risk, and compliance consulting. It offers it services under the Robert Half brand name. The company was formerly known as Robert Half International Inc. and changed its name to Robert Half Inc. in July 2023. Robert Half Inc. was founded in 1948 and is headquartered in Menlo Park, California.

### 20. [Appoints Korea Managing Director, Confirms Plans to Enter South Korean Market](https://ct.moreover.com/?a=60767544903&p=21l&v=1&x=Rmd5366ZByouCvB2dFqTlg)

- **Coverage Snippet:** XPENG INC ("XPEV-N") - Appoints Korea Managing Director, Confirms Plans to Enter South - Korean Market //st * XPENG today announced the appointment of Lee Man-sik, a 30-year automotive industry veteran with a proven track record in sales and commercial operations, as Managing Director of XPENG Motors Korea, effective December 1, 2026. * The appointment marks XPENG's planned entry into the South Korean market. Details on launch timing and local business operations will be announced at a later date.

XPeng Inc. ("XPENG" or "the Company), a leading global Physical AI company, today announced the appointment of Lee Man-sik as Managing Director of XPENG Motors Korea, as the company prepares to bring its smart vehicles to one of Asia's most sophisticated automotive markets.

Mr. Lee brings more than three decades of automotive industry experience. Having begun his career in engineering, he has built expertise across sales, marketing, product strategy, brand communications, dealer network development and customer service. He joined Volvo Cars Korea in 2003 and played a pivotal role in establishing Volvo as a leading premium import brand in South Korea.

At Volvo Cars Korea, he led the development and execution of the brand's mid- to long-term growth strategy, with responsibilities spanning brand positioning, product planning, sales and marketing, nationwide dealer expansion and customer experience.

"Over the past thirty years, I have learned that sustainable growth for a premium brand rests not only on outstanding products but on trust built with customers," said Mr. Lee. "XPENG is a brand presenting new possibilities for the industry through innovative AI technology and a vision for future mobility. I am delighted to join this journey of change."

On the Korean market, he added: "Korean customers hold exceptionally high expectations for automobiles and are remarkably open to new technology and innovation. We believe XPENG's advanced technology and product competitiveness can offer them new choices and differentiated value. Above all, our goal is to build a brand customers can choose with confidence one that delivers a trustworthy experience at every step of ownership."

In his new role, Mr. Lee will lead XPENG's efforts to establish its business foundation in South Korea, drawing on his deep understanding of the local automotive market and extensive experience in premium brand operations. His remit will include developing sales and service networks, building strategic partnerships and strengthening XPENG's brand presence in the market.

XPENG's planned entry into South Korea comes as the company continues to deepen its international footprint this year. In October 2026, the company delivered 50,237 vehicles worldwide up 22% from September and 20% from a year earlier, a new monthly record. Overseas deliveries climbed to 13,326 units, surging 166% year on year to set a record of their own. The company now operates across 83 countries and regions, supported by eight R&D centers, six manufacturing bases and more than 1,200 stores worldwide. At the 2026 Paris Motor Show in October, XPENG launched its next-gen AI flagship SUV, the G9L, for the European market and showcased its latest intelligent driving technologies, while expanding opportunities for global technology partnerships. XPENG aims to bring that same differentiated technology and customer experience to South Korea, and details on launch timing and local operations will be shared in due course.

About XPENG

XPENG is a leading global Physical AI company, dedicated to bringing artificial intelligence into the physical world to reshape future mobility and smart living. Through in-house R&D, XPENG has developed a full-stack Physical AI architecture spanning Turing AI chips, world foundation models, and highly integrated software and hardware applications. This unified technology foundation of XPENG powers an expansive product portfolio of smart EVs, robotaxis, and humanoid robots, advancing the deployment of Physical AI at scale. Headquartered in Guangzhou, China, XPENG is dual-primary listed on the New York Stock Exchange and the Hong Kong Stock Exchange. With global capabilities across R&D, manufacturing, sales, and services, XPENG drives continuous technological innovation and fosters an open Physical AI ecosystem, making life smarter, safer, and better for users worldwide. For more information, please visit https://www.xpeng.com/.

For media inquiries:

XPENG PR Department

Email: [emailprotected]

SOURCE XPENG

- **Coverage Snippet:** Asian stock markets are trading mixed on Monday, following the broadly negative cues from Wall Street on Friday, amid weakness in technology stock which mirrored their peers on tech-heavy Nasdaq on renewed concerns about the AI trade. Markets also keep an eye on the ongoing discussions between Qatari mediators and Iranian officials to end hostilities in the Middle East which could ease supply concerns. Asian markets closed mixed on Friday.

The Australian stock market is notably lower on Monday, reversing some of the gains in the previous two sessions, following the broadly negative cues from Wall Street on Friday. The benchmark S&P/ASX 200 index is falling to near the 8,850.00 level, with weakness in gold mining and financial stocks partially offset by gains in energy stocks.

The benchmark S&P/ASX 200 Index is losing 56.60 points or 0.64 percent to 8,851.50, after hitting a low of 8,851.10 earlier. The broader All Ordinaries Index is down 56.60 points or 0.63 percent to 8,968.80. Australian stocks closed modestly higher on Friday.

Among the major miners, BHP Group and Rio Tinto are losing more than 1 percent each, while Mineral Resources is gaining almost 1 percent and Fortescue is edging up 0.3 percent.

Oil stocks are mostly higher. Woodside Energy, Beach energy and Santos are gaining almost 1 percent each, while Origin Energy is edging up 0.2 percent.

Among tech stocks, Afterpay owner Block is gaining almost 1 percent, Zip is up almost 1 percent and Appen is surging more than 8 percent, while WiseTech Global is losing more than 2 percent and Xero is declining almost 3 percent.

Gold miners are mostly lower. Genesis Minerals is down almost 4 percent, Evolution Mining is losing more than 1 percent and Northern Star Resources is declining almost 4 percent, while Resolute Mining and Newmont are slipping almost 3 percent each.

Among the big four banks, Commonwealth Bank and National Australia Bank are losing almost 1 percent each, while ANZ Banking and Westpac are edging down 0.2 to 0.3 percent each.

In other news, shares in KMD Brands are surging more than 8 percent after the Kathmandu owner confirmed an exclusivity agreement with US-based brand management and investment firm Bluestar Alliance regarding a potential buyout transaction.

In economic news, the manufacturing sector in Australia continued to contract in October, and at a steady pace, the latest survey from S&P Global revealed on Monday with a Performance of Manufacturing Index score of 49.6. That was unchanged from the September reading, and it remained beneath the boom-or-bust line of 50 that separates expansion from contraction.

Australia's Melbourne Institute Monthly Inflation Gauge rose 0.5 percent on month in October 2026, up from a 0.3 percent increase in the previous month and marking a fourth straight monthly gain. Annual headline inflation accelerated to a six-month high of 4.3 percent in September, remaining well above the RBA's 2 to 3 percent target range.

Meanwhile, the total number of building permits issued in Australia was up a seasonally adjusted 5.1 percent on month in September, the Australian Bureau of Statistics said on Monday - coming in at 17,948. That beat forecasts for an increase of 1.8 percent following the upwardly revised 5.2 percent contraction in August (originally -6.1 percent). Total building approvals were down 0.8 percent on year.

In the currency market, the Aussie dollar is trading at $0.702 on Monday.

The Japanese stock market is slightly lower on Monday, extending the losses in the previous session, following the broadly negative cues from Wall Street on Friday. The Nikkei 225 is falling below the 63,250 level, with weakness in automakers and financial stocks partially offset by gains in exporter stocks.

The benchmark Nikkei 225 Index closed the morning session at 63,095.11, down 195.00 points or 0.31 percent, after hitting a low of 62,712.91 earlier. Japanese shares ended sharply lower on Friday.

Market heavyweight SoftBank Group is declining almost 2 percent, while Uniqlo operator Fast Retailing is advancing more than 2 percent. Among automakers, Honda is edging down 0.3 percent and Toyota is losing almost 1 percent.

In the tech space, Advantest is gaining more than 1 percent and Tokyo Electron is jumping more than 6 percent, while Screen Holdings is plunging more than 17 percent following downbeat financial results for the second quarter and full-year guidance.

In the banking sector, Sumitomo Mitsui Financial is declining almost 2 percent, while Mizuho Financial and Mitsubishi UFJ Financial are losing more than 1 percent each.

The major exporters are mostly higher. Mitsubishi Electric is advancing almost 4 percent, Panasonic is surging more than 5 percent and Sony is edging up 0.5 percent, while Canon is losing almost 1 percent.

Among the other major losers, Kioxia Holdings is plunging more than 13 percent, Alps Alpine is tumbling more than 10 percent, Ibiden is sliding more than 6 percent and Sumitomo Electric Industries is slipping more than 5 percent, while NTN, Taiyo Yuden and TDK are declining almost 5 percent each. Hoya is losing more than 4 percent and Rakuten Group is down almost 4 percent, while Sumco and Seiko Epson are declining more than 3 percent each.

Conversely, NGK and Sapporo Holdings are soaring more than 7 percent each, while Sumitomo Pharma is jumping more than 6 percent and Nippon Electric Glass is surging almost 5 percent, while East Japan Railway, Daiichi Sankyo and Mitsubishi Electric are gaining almost 3 percent each.

In economic news, the manufacturing sector in Japan continued to expand in October, albeit at a slower pace, the latest survey from S&P Global revealed on Monday with a Performance of Manufacturing Index score of 53.3. That was down from 54.1 in September although it remains above the boom-or-bust line of 50 that separates expansion from contraction.

In the currency market, the U.S. dollar is trading in the higher 157 yen-range on Monday.

Elsewhere in Asia, South Korea is up 1.1 percent, while China and Hong Kong are up 0.2 percent each. New Zealand, Singapore, Taiwan and Indonesia are lower by between 0.5 and 1.3 percent each. Malaysia is relatively flat.

On Wall Street, stocks saw even further downside over the course of the trading day on Friday after moving sharply lower early in the session. The major averages all moved notably lower, with the tech-heavy Nasdaq once again posting a particularly steep loss.

The major regained some ground going into the end of the day but remained sharply lower. The Nasdaq plunged 747.73 points or 2.9 percent to 25,437.00, the S&P 500 tumbled 140.32 points or 1.8 percent to 7,580.07 and the Dow slumped 572.25 points or 1.1 percent to 51,770.31.

Meanwhile, the major European markets ended the day mixed. While the U.K.'s FTSE 100 Index crept up by 0.1 percent, the French CAC 40 Index closed just below the unchanged line and the German DAX Index slid by 0.9 percent.

Crude oil prices spiked on Friday on increased supply disruption concerns, with media reports suggesting renewed U.S. military action against Iran this week. West Texas Intermediate crude Oil for December delivery was up $2.39 or 2.34 percent at $104.39 per barrel.

In the tech space, Advantest is tumbling almost 9 percent and Tokyo Electron is slipping almost 5 percent, while Screen Holdings is plunging almost 8 percent.

In the banking sector, Sumitomo Mitsui Financial and Mitsubishi UFJ Financial are losing more than 1 percent each, while Mizuho Financial is declining almost 3 percent.

Among the major exporters, Mitsubishi Electric and Panasonic are losing more than 4 percent each, while Sony is gaining almost 2 percent and Canon is edging up 0.3 percent.

Among other major losers, Fujitsu plummeting almost 16 percent, Ibiden is plunging more than 15 percent and Lasertec is tumbling almost 12 percent, while Kioxia Holdings and Murata Manufacturing are sliding more than 9 percent each. Tokyo Electric Power, Taiyo Yuden, TDK and Kokusai Electric are slipping more than 7 percent each. Chubu Electric Power, Resonac Holdings and Sumco are declining almost 7 percent each, while Mitsui Kinzoku is losing more than 6 percent.

Conversely, Konami Group is soaring more than 18 percent and Oriental Land is jumping more than 7 percent, while ANA Holdings, Capcom and BANDAI NAMCO are surging more than 6 percent each. Nomura Research Institute is advancing almost 6 percent, while Shiseido and Nitori Holdings are advancing almost 5 percent each. Nippon Yusen K.K. and Ryohin Keikaku are adding more than 4 percent each. Dentsu Group, Japan Airlines, NEXON, BayCurrent and Toho are up more than 3 percent each.

In economic news, the Bank of Japan will wrap up its monetary policy meeting on Friday and then announce its decision on interest rates. The BoJ is expected to keep its benchmark lending rate unchanged at 1.25 percent.

Retail sales in Japan advanced 2.3 percent on year in September 2026, below market expectations for a 3.3 percent rise and easing from an upwardly revised 2.9 percent increase in the previous month, marking the slowest growth in three months. It was the seventh consecutive month of growth in retail sales. On a monthly basis, retail sales dropped 0.7 percent in September, following a revised 0.9 percent fall in August, marking the second consecutive monthly decline.

Japan's unemployment rate edged down to 2.4 percent in September 2026, below market expectations and August's figure of 2.5 percent. The number of unemployed fell by 100 thousand to 1.67 million, while employment increased by 340 thousand to a four-month high of 68.68 million. Meanwhile, the jobs-to-applicants ratio rose to 1.19 from 1.18 in each of the previous three months, exceeding consensus of 1.18.

Core consumer prices in Tokyo's central wards increased 2.6 percent on year in October 2026, following a 2.7 percent rise in September and in line with expectations. It was the first deceleration in five months, following the fastest increase since November 2025 in September.

Japan's industrial production increased by 2.2 percent month-over-month in September 2026, reversing a 2.4 percent decline in the previous month and surpassing market estimates of 1.3 percent, preliminary data showed. It was the fastest growth since January. On an annual basis, production rose 1.9 percent, down from 2.7 percent in August, marking the softest growth in four months.

In the currency market, the U.S. dollar is trading in the lower 157 yen-range on Friday.

Elsewhere in Asia, South Korea and Taiwan are tumbling 5.4 and 3.2 percent, respectively. Indonesia is down 1.8 percent, while China, Singapore and Malaysia are lower by between 0.2 and 0.8 percent each. New Zealand and Hong Kong are up 0.2 and 1.2 percent, respectively.

On Wall Street, stocks moved lower during trading on Thursday, after trending higher over the past few sessions. The tech-heavy Nasdaq showed a particularly steep drop on the day.

The Nasdaq tumbled 365.13 points or 1.4 percent to 26,184.72, giving back ground after surging by 2.3 percent over the three previous sessions. The S&P 500 also dipped 23.59 points or 0.3 percent to 7,720.39, while the Dow fell 132.54 points or 0.3 percent to 52,342.56.

Meanwhile, the major European markets all moved to the upside on the day. While the U.K.'s FTSE 100 Index rose by 0.3 percent, the French CAC 40 Index shot up by 1.1 percent and the German DAX Index surged by 1.3 percent.

Crude oil prices moved lower on Thursday on expectations of a possible de-escalation in the U.S.-Iran conflict as Qatar presses Iran to negotiate with the U.S. West Texas Intermediate crude for December delivery was down $0.79 or 0.77 percent at $101.99 per barrel.

UNITED STATES EDITION

30 October 2026 08:45 EDT

Appen Ltd. (ASX:APX) finds itself in the spotlight as artificial intelligence spending continues to evolve, particularly following Anthropic's recent listing filing. As of the 28th of October 2026, the Australian data services provider has become increasingly relevant in discussions surrounding the high-quality data that AI developers require to train their models effectively.

Appen supplies crucial data services, including collection, labelling, and evaluation, leveraging a vast network of human contributors. This human-checked data is essential for model developers, who rely on it to train systems, assess accuracy, and identify weaknesses before public release. Unlike technology licensors, Appen's revenue model hinges on customer outsourcing decisions and the margins it can achieve in the process.

The recent attention on Anthropic's filing highlights the heavy financial commitments needed for advanced AI model development, signaling a sustained demand for quality data services. While this may bode well for Appen, it also indicates that major players in the AI space may increasingly build in-house capabilities, which could pressure pricing for data services. Moreover, Meta's fund-raising efforts to support its AI initiatives suggest that even the most resource-rich companies are looking externally to bolster their data supply chains.

Appen's share price has seen significant fluctuations this month, reflecting broader market sentiment rather than any specific company announcements. This volatility underscores how small-cap technology stocks like Appen can react sharply to shifts in AI sentiment, particularly when compared to larger players in the sector. Commentary around the company's performance often draws comparisons to other ASX-listed firms, positioning Appen as a unique case study in the AI supply chain.

Looking forward, market observers will closely monitor Appen's efforts to diversify its customer base. The company has historically relied on a small number of large clients, which poses risks if one decides to alter its strategy. As automation encroaches on simpler labelling tasks, the demand for more complex evaluation work—where human judgement remains essential—may provide Appen with new opportunities, should it successfully navigate these competitive waters.

Wed Sep 30, 2026: ExlService Reports Significant Sustainability Advances in Latest Report

ExlService Holdings, Inc. [NASDAQ: EXLS] has published its seventh annual Sustainability Report, titled 'Embedding Sustainability,' showcasing significant advancements in corporate social responsibility (CSR), emissions reductions, and responsible AI governance. The report, released on 30th September 2026, highlights EXL's commitment to integrating sustainability into its core business practices as a means to enhance long-term value for stakeholders.

In the report, dated 2026-10-01 16:41:39.309490, EXL reveals a remarkable 64% reduction in Scope 1 and 2 greenhouse gas emissions from its 2019 baseline. This achievement is complemented by an impressive 78% of the global workforce participating in various CSR initiatives, reflecting the company's dedication to fostering a culture of community engagement and environmental stewardship.

Ajay Ayyappan, executive vice president and general counsel at EXL, stated, 'At EXL, sustainability is not a side initiative; it is central to how we build a stronger, more resilient business.' He emphasized that the report embodies the company's ethos of continuous improvement, demonstrating measurable progress across all facets of its sustainability agenda, including a robust increase in community investment and the maturation of its Responsible and Secure AI program.

The 2025 Sustainability Report adheres to the Global Reporting Initiative Standards and the Sustainability Accounting Standards Board Software and IT Services Standard, ensuring transparency and accountability. Additionally, it aligns with the recommendations of the Task Force on Climate-related Financial Disclosures and maps to the United Nations Sustainable Development Goals, further solidifying EXL's commitment to global sustainability efforts.

EXL remains focused on propelling enterprises to enhance their business models through data and AI, with an emphasis on sustainable practices. As the company continues to innovate and lead in responsible corporate governance, the insights from this report may be of particular interest to investors looking to align their portfolios with companies demonstrating strong environmental, social, and governance (ESG) practices.

Wed Sep 9, 2026: EXL Launches ‘Go Beyond’ Brand, Emphasizing Data-Driven Transformation

EXLService Holdings, Inc. (NASDAQ:EXLS) has unveiled its new brand, ‘Go Beyond,' which encapsulates the company's commitment to innovation and transformation in the realms of data and artificial intelligence. This announcement comes as EXL celebrates its 20th year as a publicly traded entity, with the brand launch taking place on 10th September 2026 at a Nasdaq opening bell ceremony. The new branding aims to reflect EXL's unique position at the intersection of data, AI, industry context, and enterprise execution, driving measurable outcomes for its clients.

Rohit Kapoor, chairman and chief executive officer of EXL, emphasized that ‘Go Beyond' is not just a tagline but a daily standard for the company. He stated, "Our clients come to us to fundamentally reimagine how their enterprises operate. With AI transforming every industry, we've built something rare; a company with domain depth to know what questions to ask and data and AI capabilities to answer them at scale." This strategic shift aims to further solidify EXL's standing as a leader in leveraging data and AI for business transformation.

The rebranding initiative aligns with EXL's foundational purpose of finding better ways to serve its clients and symbolizes its ongoing growth and adaptability. Since its foray into data and analytics in 2006, EXL has consistently pushed the boundaries of traditional industry norms. This includes the recent acquisition of iMerit in August 2026, which has expanded its capabilities in advanced AI model training and frontier AI development. Today, over 60% of EXL's revenues are derived from data and AI-driven services, showcasing its market-leading growth trajectory.

Shirley Macbeth, chief marketing officer of EXL, remarked, "For 27 years, EXL has been built on a simple idea: We find a better way. ‘Go Beyond' is the brand that captures that. It's an invitation to our clients and employees to push the boundaries of what's possible and a reflection of the company EXL has become, a global data and AI leader that combines deep domain and context expertise with trusted execution to drive outcomes that matter." This statement underscores EXL's focus on fostering an innovative culture that prioritizes client success.

EXL continues to serve a diverse clientele that includes leading corporations across various sectors such as insurance, healthcare, banking, and capital markets. As the company embarks on this new branding journey, stakeholders will be keenly observing how ‘Go Beyond' translates into tangible outcomes and strategic advantages for clients in an increasingly competitive landscape. For more insights into EXL's transformation and the ‘Go Beyond' brand in action, interested parties can visit the company's official website.

Thu Aug 20, 2026: ExlService Holdings Expands Credit Capacity: A New Era for AI Investments?

ExlService Holdings, Inc. has taken a significant step in its financial strategy by entering into a new US$1.00 billion senior secured credit agreement with a syndicate led by PNC Bank, as announced on August 18, 2026. This move replaces its previous Citibank facility and extends revolving and term loan maturities to August 18, 2031.

The new credit facility is designed to provide ExlService greater balance sheet flexibility, allowing the company to fund acquisitions, technology investments, and share repurchases. It features leverage-linked pricing and covenants that impose defined limits on debt and interest coverage, which enhances the company's ability to pursue its ambitious goals in the AI sector.

As ExlService Holdings pivots from traditional outsourcing to a focus on data and AI, the new US$1.00 billion credit line appears to support the ongoing narrative of AI-driven growth. This expanded credit capacity is expected to bolster ExlService's strategy to secure AI-led deal wins while managing the associated risks, particularly those arising from rising wage inflation and compliance costs, which could complicate pricing power.

The recent acquisition of iMerit, which has brought its CEO into ExlService's executive team, further underscores the importance of scaling AI data services. Together with the larger credit facility, this acquisition highlights the company's commitment to enhancing its capabilities in this high-demand sector, while also raising concerns related to execution risks in integrating new talent and maintaining resilient operating margins.

Analysts are optimistic about ExlService Holdings' growth potential, projecting revenues of approximately US$3.2 billion and earnings of around US$374.5 million by 2029. The new credit facility could reinforce this growth outlook; however, if cost pressures intensify more than anticipated, it may lead to a reassessment of the feasibility of meeting these targets.

Wed Sep 9, 2026: EXL Launches ‘Go Beyond’ Brand, Emphasizing Data-Driven Transformation

The new branding aims to reflect EXL's unique position at the intersection of data, AI, industry context, and enterprise execution, driving measurable outcomes for its clients.

Rohit Kapoor, chairman and chief executive officer of EXL, emphasized that ‘Go Beyond' is not just a tagline but a daily standard for the company. He stated, "Our clients come to us to fundamentally reimagine how their enterprises operate. The rebranding initiative aligns with EXL's foundational purpose of finding better ways to serve its clients and symbolizes its ongoing growth and adaptability. Since its foray into data and analytics in 2006, EXL has consistently pushed the boundaries of traditional industry norms. Thu Aug 20, 2026: ExlService Holdings Expands Credit Capacity: A New Era for AI Investments?

ExlService Holdings, Inc. has taken a significant step in its financial strategy by entering into a new US$1.00 billion senior secured credit agreement with a syndicate led by PNC Bank, as announced on August 18, 2026. This move replaces its previous Citibank facility and extends revolving and term loan maturities to August 18, 2031.

The new credit facility is designed to provide ExlService greater balance sheet flexibility, allowing the company to fund acquisitions, technology investments, and share repurchases. It features leverage-linked pricing and covenants that impose defined limits on debt and interest coverage, which enhances the company's ability to pursue its ambitious goals in the AI sector.

As ExlService Holdings pivots from traditional outsourcing to a focus on data and AI, the new US$1.00 billion credit line appears to support the ongoing narrative of AI-driven growth. This expanded credit capacity is expected to bolster ExlService's strategy to secure AI-led deal wins while managing the associated risks, particularly those arising from rising wage inflation and compliance costs, which could complicate pricing power.

The recent acquisition of iMerit, which has brought its CEO into ExlService's executive team, further underscores the importance of scaling AI data services. Together with the larger credit facility, this acquisition highlights the company's commitment to enhancing its capabilities in this high-demand sector, while also raising concerns related to execution risks in integrating new talent and maintaining resilient operating margins.

Analysts are optimistic about ExlService Holdings' growth potential, projecting revenues of approximately US$3.2 billion and earnings of around US$374.5 million by 2029. The new credit facility could reinforce this growth outlook; however, if cost pressures intensify more than anticipated, it may lead to a reassessment of the feasibility of meeting these targets.

### 37. [John Deere built an AI assistant to stop farmers from drowning in decades of field data](https://www.businessinsider.com/farming-technology-john-deere-ai-data-crop-planning-2026-11)
- **Outlet(s):** Business Insider | **Published:** `2026-11-02` | **Sentiment:** `NEUTRAL` | **Positioning Relevance Score:** `0.60`
- **Syndicated URLs:** [link](https://ct.moreover.com/?a=60761296639&p=21l&v=1&x=i99w4Kx-pjj7Y6xsv8jTfQ)
- **Matched Competitors:** Palantir, Appen, Turing
- **Coverage Snippet:** •John Deere's AI assistant, unveiled in September, helps farmers analyze data quickly.

•JD is embedded in John Deere's Operations Center, a digital farm management system.

•Farmers are warming to AI, but accuracy and data privacy remain concerns.

Farmers have more data than ever about their fields, equipment, and crops. But turning it into a decision could take weeks.

John Deere says its new AI assistant, JD, can do it in minutes.

The agricultural manufacturing company announced the tool in September at the Farm Progress Show in Iowa. JD is integrated within John Deere's Operations Center, which is a digital farm management system that connects farmers' field and machine data into one platform. Farmers can ask JD questions on their mobile devices, such as the best time to harvest based on past trends, or which sprayer covers the most acres per hour.

Melissa Neuendorf, the principal product manager for customer AI solutions at John Deere, told Business Insider that JD came about because farmers consistently said they were struggling to manage their abundant data.

"We're now at a point where farmers have so much data that they have a difficult time finding the insights manually themselves," Neuendorf said.

While smaller agricultural tech companies such as OneSoil, VisorPro, Arable, and AgZen are developing generative AI tools to give insights on specific equipment or crop metrics, Deere is among the first major agricultural-equipment makers to put a large language model AI assistant directly inside a platform that combines farmers' field, machine, and operational data. JD's launch shows how generative AI is becoming a central part of crop management.

"Prior to John Deere's announcement, this didn't exist in the industry," said Matt Darr, a professor in the agricultural and biosystems engineering department at Iowa State University, who was in the room when Deere announced JD.

Why farmers are considering AI tools more than ever

Farmers have been collecting data for decades. But now, modern farm equipment is outfitted with additional technology and sensors, creating large volumes of data that are difficult to keep up with.

It can be stressful for farmers who must also navigate tight margins, unpredictable weather, and limited planting windows. Plus, "most farmers aren't data scientists on the side," Darr said.

Neuendorf said dissecting vast datasets is complex and time-consuming, sometimes requiring weeks. That's where AI can help farmers: "AI provides them the capability to do a lot of that analysis in minutes," she said.

Before coming up with JD, Deere launched an AI tool last August that allowed farmers to ask questions in natural language about Deere's help documentation and operator manuals. This replaced the manual task of farmers flipping through pages of operator manuals.

The tool received positive feedback, prompting Deere to broaden its application and launch JD to answer specific questions on the spot.

Farmers can input data into the Operations Center from non-John Deere equipment if they use machinery from other manufacturers. Then, if they query JD, it can analyze all of that data to deliver answers.

The Operations Center and JD are free to use, Neuendorf said. The AI assistant is available through an early access program, in which interested customers sign up for a waitlist and are considered for participation. John Deere will make JD broadly available in mid-November

Neuendorf told Business Insider that the feedback from farmers so far has been positive.

Neuendorf said she recently spoke to a farmer who was driving past a field and noticed it had lower weed pressure, or fewer weeds present, than other fields. Through JD, the farmer was able to open John Deere's mobile app right away, ask the assistant about previous years, and understand how he managed the field differently.

Winning farmer trust in AI technology

A McKinsey global survey of 5,500 farmers, published in September, found that 72% expect generative AI to impact operations within five years, but only 17% use the tech right now.

One reason only a minority of farmers use AI is because they're worried about data privacy and how their information could impact commodity prices, Darr said.

"These are competitive businesses," Darr added. "How information is used can influence global markets and what happens to global commodity pricing."

John Deere said in its release announcing JD that it doesn't sell farm data or use it for agriculture commodity trading or speculation.

Katya Kheistver, the chief product and operating officer of OneSoil, an agricultural technology company, said farmers won't trust AI if it delivers generic advice rather than insights tied to specific, real field data. Farmers often start their use of AI by checking the tech's outputs against what they already know. The farmer "watches whether the answer lines up with reality," and if it does so consistently, trust grows, Kheistver said.

The stakes are high if farmers make decisions based on AI-generated answers because they have "one chance a year to grow a crop," Darr said. They need to be able to trust that an AI tool is accurate.

JD meshes high-quality datasets with advanced LLMs, Darr added, which sets the AI tool apart and promises a high degree of accuracy.

Neuendorf said JD is configured to notify farmers when field data is missing, rather than making inaccurate assumptions or generating false advice.

This configuration also encourages farmers to input additional data, so JD can derive more insights and help farmers "make better decisions for the next season," Neuendorf said.

- **Coverage Snippet:** TELUS CORP ("T-T;TU-N") - New TELUS collective agreement ratified by the United - Steelworkers membership

TELUS today announced that team members represented by the Telecommunications Workers Union, United Steelworkers Local 1944 (USW) bargaining unit have ratified the new agreement reached by the parties earlier this month. This new agreement, which has a term of April 1, 2027 to December 31, 2031, will apply to more than 3,000 TELUS team members nationally.

"Thank you to our team members for supporting this agreement and to both bargaining teams for their constructive work," said Victor Dodig, President and CEO of TELUS. "Reaching an agreement well ahead of the current contract's expiry provides stability for our team members and our business, allowing us to focus together on delivering the reliable service and outstanding experiences our customers count on."

About TELUS

TELUS (TSX: T, NYSE: TU) is a leading Canadian communications technology company operating in more than 45 countries and generating over $20 billion in annual revenue with more than 17 million customer connections through our advanced suite of broadband services for consumers, businesses and the public sector. TELUS is passionate about putting our customers and communities first, leading the way globally in client service excellence and social capitalism. The TELUS PureFibre and 5G networks connect Canadians at home, at work and in the communities where they live.

TELUS Health is enhancing approximately 159 million lives across 200 countries and territories through innovative preventive medicine and well-being technologies. TELUS Agriculture &Consumer Goods utilizes digital technologies and data insights to optimize the connection between producers and consumers. TELUS Digital specializes in digital customer experiences and future-focused digital transformations that deliver value for their global clients.

Since 2000, TELUS and our team members have contributed more than $1.85 billion in cash, in-kind contributions, time and programs to communities across Canada and around the world.

For more information, visit telus.com.

For more information, please contact:

Wed Sep 30, 2026: ExlService Reports Significant Sustainability Advances in Latest Report

ExlService Holdings, Inc. [NASDAQ: EXLS] has published its seventh annual Sustainability Report, titled 'Embedding Sustainability,' showcasing significant advancements in corporate social responsibility (CSR), emissions reductions, and responsible AI governance. The report, released on 30th September 2026, highlights EXL's commitment to integrating sustainability into its core business practices as a means to enhance long-term value for stakeholders.

In the report, dated 2026-10-01 16:41:39.309490, EXL reveals a remarkable 64% reduction in Scope 1 and 2 greenhouse gas emissions from its 2019 baseline. This achievement is complemented by an impressive 78% of the global workforce participating in various CSR initiatives, reflecting the company's dedication to fostering a culture of community engagement and environmental stewardship.

Ajay Ayyappan, executive vice president and general counsel at EXL, stated, 'At EXL, sustainability is not a side initiative; it is central to how we build a stronger, more resilient business.' He emphasized that the report embodies the company's ethos of continuous improvement, demonstrating measurable progress across all facets of its sustainability agenda, including a robust increase in community investment and the maturation of its Responsible and Secure AI program.

The 2025 Sustainability Report adheres to the Global Reporting Initiative Standards and the Sustainability Accounting Standards Board Software and IT Services Standard, ensuring transparency and accountability. Additionally, it aligns with the recommendations of the Task Force on Climate-related Financial Disclosures and maps to the United Nations Sustainable Development Goals, further solidifying EXL's commitment to global sustainability efforts.

EXL remains focused on propelling enterprises to enhance their business models through data and AI, with an emphasis on sustainable practices. As the company continues to innovate and lead in responsible corporate governance, the insights from this report may be of particular interest to investors looking to align their portfolios with companies demonstrating strong environmental, social, and governance (ESG) practices.
